2026-10-10T05:30:52.4763534Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Lengthen EAGLE3 to steps4/draft5 on the decode-bound P3/D1 c72

Why: at ~60 running requests the single P3/D1 decode worker limits
goodput (ed8c534: 3313.0 good tok/s, ITL 17.18 ms, accept length 2.23).
The bounded decode capture (f84ea21) and the one-GPU probe (0e65dee) show
that per verify step MoE streams the same FP8 weights regardless of token
count, and FA3 verify attention packs 4 tokens x 16 Q heads = 64 rows into
one 128-row tile per KV head, so a fifth verify token (80 rows) should
not add KV reads. The extra cost is one more draft decode step plus a few
GEMM rows, while the accept length should rise by roughly the probability
that all three current drafts are accepted times the fourth acceptance
(~0.13 tokens, ~6%). The earlier choice of steps3/draft4 was made at c40
with ~12 running requests per decode worker, where fixed draft/verify
overhead weighed more.

Change (runtime only): configs/aspire-serving.json speculation steps 3 -> 4
and draft_tokens 4 -> 5 (topk 1, same BF16 EAGLE3 draft). Everything else
equals ed8c534: P3/D1, decode pool 393216, c72, FA3, page size 1, Triton
decode MoE, FP8 KV, same image lineage. Output tokens stay target-verified.

Judge by accept length, ITL at comparable running batch and good tok/s
against ed8c534; different node pair likely, so not a matched pair.

Budget: myprojects 59010014 balance 11913.350 SU, In Doubt 1024 before
this push. This job reserves at most 1024 SU.

Validation: full CPU pytest (argv fixture with steps4/draft5) and Ruff.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

2026-10-10T05:34:21.5130000Z Expanded: (true && (!contains('[cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Lengthen EAGLE3 to steps4/draft5 on the decode-bound P3/D1 c72

Why: at ~60 running requests the single P3/D1 decode worker limits
goodput (ed8c534: 3313.0 good tok/s, ITL 17.18 ms, accept length 2.23).
The bounded decode capture (f84ea21) and the one-GPU probe (0e65dee) show
that per verify step MoE streams the same FP8 weights regardless of token
count, and FA3 verify attention packs 4 tokens x 16 Q heads = 64 rows into
one 128-row tile per KV head, so a fifth verify token (80 rows) should
not add KV reads. The extra cost is one more draft decode step plus a few
GEMM rows, while the accept length should rise by roughly the probability
that all three current drafts are accepted times the fourth acceptance
(~0.13 tokens, ~6%). The earlier choice of steps3/draft4 was made at c40
with ~12 running requests per decode worker, where fixed draft/verify
overhead weighed more.
